  - (ST2) The limit $L\to\infty$ of the ratio exists in every order of PT: the pinch-pole singularity of infinitely long space-like links cancels between the staple self-interactions and $\sqrt{Z_E}$; ultrasoft modes cancel after resummation of dipole self-interactions.
  - (ST3) On the lattice, $\sqrt{Z_E}$ together with the short-distance hadron matrix element removes all UV divergences (RI/MOM fails to remove all linear divergences); same-dimension mixing as in `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(b).
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-quasi-tmd` — 准 TMD** (no tag).
  - In our conventions (§F0.3 rows 1–3; `Ji:2019ewn`, `Liu:2022nnk` §3, `Ebert:2022fmh` converted):
    $$\tilde f_\Gamma(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta_z)=\lim_{L\to\infty}N_\Gamma\int\frac{dz}{4\pi}e^{ixP^zz}\frac{\langle P|\bar\psi(\frac z2\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2)\,\Gamma\,\mathcal W_z(\tfrac z2\hat e_z+\tfrac{\vec b_T}2,-\tfrac z2\hat e_z-\tfrac{\vec b_T}2;L)\,\psi(-\frac z2\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2)|P\rangle}{\sqrt{Z_E(2L,b_T,\mu)}},\qquad\zeta_z=(2xP^z)^2,$$
    $\mathcal W_z$ = the staple from $-\frac z2\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2$ along $+\hat e_z$ to $L\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2$, a transverse link to $L\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2$, and back along $-\hat e_z$ to $\frac z2\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2$ (DY: staple toward $+\hat z$; SIDIS: toward $-\hat z$); $Z_E(2L,b_T,\mu)=\frac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle0|W_\perp\mathcal W_z(\vec b_T;2L)|0\rangle$ (rectangular $2L\times b_T$ loop); $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ or $\gamma^z$ (any combination except $\gamma^0-\gamma^z$). The quasi-TMD has **no rapidity divergence**: $P^z$ acts as an off-light-cone rapidity regulator. ESSZ's "proper" quasi-TMD and their Lorentz-invariant correlator $\Omega(b,P,\epsilon,\eta v,\delta)$ covering the quasi, Collins, large-rapidity (LR), JMY and MHENS schemes (`Ebert:2022fmh`, read) are mentioned in text. Figure `fig:lamet-lattice-geometries`(b),(c); note that `fig:tmd-staple` shows light-like staples (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §1 note).
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-staple, def:lattice-quasi, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-soft-offlc` — 离光锥软函数（Ji–Liu–Liu 归一化）** (no tag).
  - Time-like $v=\gamma(1,\beta,\vec0_\perp)$, $v'=\gamma'(1,-\beta',\vec0_\perp)$ in $(t,z,\vec b_\perp)$, $\beta=\tanh Y$, $\beta'=\tanh Y'$; $W_v(\xi;t)=W[\xi,\xi-\frac t\gamma v]$ (our convention); staples $\mathcal W_v(\vec b_\perp,t)=W_v(0;t)W_\perp W_v^\dagger(\vec b_\perp;t)$;
    $$W(t,t',b_\perp,Y,Y')=\tfrac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle\Omega|T[\mathcal W^\dagger_{v'}(\vec b_\perp,t')\mathcal W_v(\vec b_\perp,t)]|\Omega\rangle,\quad Z(2t,b_\perp,Y)=W(t,t,b_\perp,Y,-Y),\quad S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=\lim_{t,t'\to\infty}\frac{W(t,t',b_\perp,Y,Y')}{\sqrt{Z(2t,b_\perp,Y)Z(2t',b_\perp,Y')}},$$
    $\overline{\rm MS}$-renormalized. $S$ depends on $Y+Y'$ only (boost invariance). One loop: $S^{(1)}=a[2-2(Y+Y')]L_b+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$ (`% VF-YZL-2`; = the time-like, self-energy-included case $\sigma=1$ of `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, VF-TMD-8).
  - Writer trap (print as a footnote): with Collins' space-like lines and self-interactions excluded (`def:tmd-soft`) the rapidity-independent constant vanishes at one loop ($\sigma=0$); individual soft functions are regulator dependent, only combinations are physical (critic C10).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-wilson, def:tmd-soft, lem:tmd-soft-oneloop}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` — 离光锥软函数的大快度结构** [A]. Register entry: Part VII annex §F1.1 (= `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §3).
  - (SR) As $Y+Y'\to\infty$: $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$, with $\tilde K$ the Collins–Soper kernel of `prop:tmd-evolution` and $\mathcal D$ finite.
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-reduced-soft` — 约化软函数 $S_r$** (no tag).
  - $S_r(b_\perp,\mu)\equiv e^{-\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)}=\lim_{Y+Y'\to\infty}e^{(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)}/S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')$; one loop $S_r=1-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi L_b$ (`% VF-YZL-2`). Called the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ in Ch23, where its on-light-cone definition, scheme independence and anomalous dimension $\mu^2\frac{d\ln S_r}{d\mu^2}=\gamma_{HH}$ are established (pointer: `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft`). $S_r\ne e^{-\mathcal D_{\rm Collins}}$ (trap above).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, asm:lamet-soft-rapidity}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-order-limits` — 极限次序** [A]. Register entry: Part VII annex §F1.1 (= `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §3).
  - (OL1) $\varepsilon\to0$ commutes with $L\to\infty$ and with the off-light-cone rapidity regulator limit $\tilde\eta\to\infty$ for the ratio $\Omega/\sqrt S$ (self-energies cancel because the staple of $\Omega$ is half that of $S$; exchanges between the halves are exponentially suppressed; eikonal poles $1/(n_B\cdot k)$ give the same result with either regulator — verified at one loop).
  - (OL2) The large-rapidity and the Collins schemes differ only in the order of $\varepsilon\to0$ and $y_B\to-\infty$; by asymptotic freedom this is a UV effect: $f^{\rm LR}=C_q(\sqrt{\zeta_{\rm LR}}/2,\mu)f^{\rm TMD}+O(y_B^ke^{y_B})$, with $C_q$ independent of $y_n-y_B$ (otherwise the non-perturbative CS kernel would enter).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-collins}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` — 准 TMD 因子化** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $b_T\gg1/(xP^z)$, $\zeta_z\gg\Lambda^2$, at leading power,
    $$\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta_z)\sqrt{S_r(b_T,\mu)}=H\Big(\frac{\zeta_z}{\mu^2}\Big)\exp\Big[\frac12\tilde K(b_T,\mu)\ln\frac{\zeta_z}\zeta\Big]f^{\rm TMD}(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta)\Big\{1+O\Big(\frac{\Lambda^2}{\zeta_z},\frac{M^2}{\zeta_z},\frac1{b_T^2\zeta_z}\Big)\Big\},$$
    $f^{\rm TMD}$ the Collins TMD (`def:tmd-collins`), $2\zeta\partial_\zeta\ln f^{\rm TMD}=\tilde K$. The relation is **multiplicative in $x$** (no convolution), flavour diagonal (no quark–gluon or flavour mixing), spin independent ($H$ depends only on the colour representation), and holds for quarks and gluons. Momentum evolution: $2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln\tilde f=\tilde K+G$, $G=2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln H$. Equivalent ESSZ form: $\tilde f^{[s]}_{i/h}(x,\vec b_T,\mu,\tilde\zeta,x\tilde P^z)=C_i(x\tilde P^z,\mu)\exp[\frac12\gamma^i_\zeta\ln\frac{\tilde\zeta}\zeta]f^{[s]}_{i/h}(x,\vec b_T,\mu,\zeta)\{1+O[\frac1{(x\tilde P^zb_T)^2},\frac{\Lambda^2}{(x\tilde P^z)^2}]\}$ with $\gamma_\zeta=\tilde K$ (their proper quasi-TMD contains $S_r$). (b)（单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）$H=1+a(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta)$, $G^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(1-L_\zeta)$, and the coefficient of $\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)$ is fixed to $\frac12$ by the one-loop expressions (`ex:lamet-qtmd-oneloop`). (c)（两圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）the NNLO coefficient (`delRio:2023pse`, read) agrees with the two-loop heavy–light Sudakov kernel of Ji–Liu–Su (values in Ch23 `prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`, pointer). `% VF-LAM-15`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-bridge`、`asm:intro-composite`（MA0、MA1、MA2）。
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:lamet-reduced-soft, def:tmd-collins, prop:tmd-evolution, asm:lamet-staple, asm:lamet-soft-rapidity, asm:lamet-order-limits, lem:tmd-soft-oneloop, prop:tmd-evolution-oneloop, prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.7 pp) with independent routes: **(A)** Ji–Liu–Liu leading regions (`Ji:2019ewn`, read; a "sketch of mathematical proof", details "to a future publication"): collinear subgraphs identical to the light-front TMD; soft radiation between the fast colour charge and the staple captured by the off-light-cone soft function; hard subgraphs at $\zeta_z$ localized at the two quark–link vertices, since any hard momentum flowing between $0$ and $\vec b_T$ costs a power of $1/P^z$ ⇒ no convolution, in sharp contrast with the collinear quasi-PDF. **(B)** Ebert–Schindler–Stewart–Zhao (`Ebert:2022fmh`, read; all orders, unpolarized quark in detail, gluons and spin by "minor, straightforward modifications"): (1) quasi, LR and Collins beam functions as Fourier transforms of one Lorentz-invariant correlator $\Omega$, leading power keeps the $P^\mu\Omega$ structure; (2) boost by $y_B$: $\tilde b^+=-b^-e^{2y_B}\to0$, $\tilde b^z\to0$, $y_{\tilde P}=y_P-y_B\to\infty$, so the quasi correlator equals the LR correlator with finite-length links up to terms suppressed at large proton rapidity; (3) (OL1); (4) (OL2) ⇒ $C_q=C_q(\zeta_{\rm LR}/\mu^2,\alpha_s)$; (5) no mixing: the quark fields stay space-like separated, so $y_B\to-\infty$ cannot generate a UV counterterm contracting flavour indices (contrast: in the collinear quasi-PDF the fields approach the light cone and mixing occurs); cross-check through the JMY scheme (space-like ↔ time-like continuation). **(C)** SCET (`Vladimirov:2020ofp`, abstract): factorized expression with a "nonperturbative lattice related factor" that cancels in ratios; an SCET$_{\rm II}$ derivation with a one-loop check (A04 item (ii)). **(D)** Coulomb-gauge quasi-TMDs (`Zhao:2023ptv`, abstract): reduce to standard TMD correlators under a principal-value prescription; T-even TMDs only. **(E)** Liu's Collins-style power counting: Ch23 `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount` (pointer).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (several independent arguments); the decisive step (OL2) is an order-of-limits argument, not a diagram-by-diagram subtraction proof; power-counting steps give E-S statements; no dedicated published Glauber/soft-exchange analysis for finite-length staples (literature-absence claim A06 item (2): holds — no title hit; the full-text hits only mention Glauber modes); the hadronic statement needs `asm:intro-bridge`. Over-claim to avoid: 「准 TMD 因子化已严格证明」.
  - *Verification outcome.* The printed exponent $e^{\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)}$ of `Ji:2019ewn` eq. (22), `JiEtAl2021` eqs. (209)–(210) (and `Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (53), (75); `Ji:2021znw` only in its arXiv v1 — the journal version is correct and its footnote 1 notes the omission; verify s1, `verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md` §3) is wrong by a factor 2 (claim E02 confirmed; App Q, row Q.11); print the correct form.
* **例 `ex:lamet-qtmd-oneloop` — 单圈检验：指数中的因子 $\frac12$** (no tag). With $a=\alpha_sC_F/(2\pi)$: $\tilde f^{(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+L_b(\frac52-L_\zeta)-\frac32-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta]$, $f^{{\rm TMD}(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+(\frac32-\ln\frac\zeta{\mu^2})L_b+\frac12-\frac{\pi^2}{12}]$, $S_r^{(1)}=\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$; insert into $\ln\tilde f+\frac12\ln S_r-\ln H-c\tilde K\ln\frac{\zeta_z}\zeta-\ln f^{\rm TMD}$: the $L_bL_\zeta$ and $L_b\ln(\zeta/\mu^2)$ terms cancel iff $c=\frac12$; the constants give $H^{(1)}$ ($-\frac32=-2+\frac12$). The CS ratio of `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` then returns $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$. Checks: LAMET s4, YZLIU `check_qtmd_1loop.py`, spec-v2 drafter by hand (re-derived for this outline). `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`. 0.3 pp.
* **命题 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` — 格点 Collins–Soper 核** [A] (v1 label and tag).
  - *Statement.* At fixed $x$, $b_T$ and two momenta $P_1^z,P_2^z$:
    $$\tilde K(b_T,\mu)=\frac1{\ln(P_1^z/P_2^z)}\ln\frac{C(\mu,xP_2^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_1^z)}{C(\mu,xP_1^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_2^z)}+\text{power corrections},\qquad C(\mu,xP^z)\equiv H\Big(\frac{(2xP^z)^2}{\mu^2}\Big),$$
    equivalently $\frac{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,1})}{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,2})}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}(\frac{\zeta_{z,1}}{\zeta_{z,2}})^{\tilde K/2}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}(\frac{P^z_1}{P^z_2})^{\tilde K}$; $S_r$ and all $\Gamma$-, flavour- and spin-independent factors cancel; the $x$ independence of the right-hand side tests leading power. (单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$) the formula returns $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$ exactly. `% VF-LAM-16`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-bridge`（MA1、MA2）。
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full given the theorem (two lines). Literature: `EbertStewartZhao2019` (read; original proposal), `Ebert:2019tvc` (abstract; RI′/MOM for staples, position-space procedure); lattice determinations (evidence): `Shanahan:2020zxr`, `Shanahan:2021tst`, `Schlemmer:2021aij`, `LatticePartonLPC:2022eev`, `LatticePartonLPC:2023pdv`, `Avkhadiev:2023poz`, `Avkhadiev:2024mgd` (abstract: "first such determination with systematic control of quark mass, operator mixing, and discretization effects"), `Bollweg:2024zet`, `Tan:2025ofx` (abstract). The slow convergence of the imaginary part in quasi-TMDWF-based extractions is explained by the linear renormalon of Ch23 (pointer).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`); M17: $\tilde K$ at $b_T\sim1/\Lambda$ is non-perturbative and the lattice supplies it only through the [A] theorem.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-tmd-ratios` — 准 TMD 之比与 TMD 之比** [A].
  - *Statement.* At fixed $(x,b_T,P^z)$, ratios of quasi-TMDs with different $\Gamma$ in the spin-universality class, different flavours or different target polarizations equal the corresponding ratios of TMDs at leading power ($S_r$, $H$ and the CS factor cancel because $H$ is spin and flavour independent and $S_r$, $\tilde K$ depend on $b_T$ only); this justifies the earlier staple "ratio" lattice programme (`Hagler:2009mb`, `Musch:2010ka`, `Musch:2011er`, `Yoon:2017qzo`) in the large-momentum limit (`Ebert:2022fmh`, `Vladimirov:2020ofp`). Spin universality: helicity and transversity quasi-TMDs have the same one-loop coefficient as the unpolarized one, argued for pretzelosity and to all orders (`Ebert:2020gxr`, abstract). Gluon quasi-TMDs: one-loop hard kernel, independent of the pinch-pole prescription (`Zhu:2022bja`, abstract). NLP quasi-TMDs (`Rodini:2022wic`, abstract): twist-2, twist-3 TMDs and a lattice-specific non-perturbative function. Ratios at **different** $b_T$ do not cancel $S_r$ and the CS factor (the frontier report's phrase "$b_T$ at fixed $x$" is a slip; never print).
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits the theorem); abstract-level items hedged.
* *Budget of §22.13.* 2.0 pp.

#### §22.14 `sec:lattice-status` (v1)

* **推论 `cor:lamet-principle-pt` — LaMET 原理的微扰内容** [A].
  - *Statement.* For partonic external states at leading power, the expansion of `conj:lamet-principle` holds order by order in renormalized perturbation theory for: non-singlet quark quasi-PDFs (`thm:lattice-lamet`), singlet quark and gluon quasi-PDFs with mixing (`prop:lamet-gluon-singlet`), quasi-GPDs and quasi-DAs (`thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`), quasi-TMDs with the reduced soft function (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`); explicit at one loop (`prop:lamet-oneloop`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`(b)) and at two loops for the non-singlet PDF kernel and the quasi-TMD hard kernel.
  - `\uses{thm:lattice-lamet, prop:lamet-gluon-singlet, thm:lamet-quasi-gpd, thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; the order-of-limits picture ("IR physics unchanged") is a heuristic; the actual arguments are the OPE route, the collinear-subtraction route and the boost/LR route.
* **注 `rem:lattice-critiques` — 批评与回应** (v1 label; content extended).
  - (a) Power-divergent mixing (`Rossi:2017muf`, `Rossi:2018zkn`, abstracts): higher moments of DIS operators mix with lower-dimensional operators on the lattice; claimed to affect equal-time correlators, reduced ITDs and current–current T-products. Responses: the only power divergence of the non-local operator is the Wilson-line self-energy, which factorizes (`Ji:2017rah`, abstract; `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); after the continuum limit at fixed $z\ne0$ the moments from non-local matrix elements are finite and agree with local twist-2 moments (`Karpie:2018zaz`, abstract; quenched test). The order of limits ($a\to0$ at fixed $z$, then $z\to0$ inside the OPE) avoids the problem; the resolution was contested in 2017–2018, with published exchanges on both sides (A06 corrected: `Rossi:2018zkn` call the counter-arguments "untenable", their later proceedings concede single moments but maintain the obstruction for the full PDF; replies `Karpie:2018zaz`, `Radyushkin:2018nbf`); no later Rossi–Testa paper was found.
  - (b) Euclidean vs Minkowski: `prop:lamet-euclid-minkowski`.
  - (c) The inverse problem: `rem:lamet-inverse-problem`.
  - (d) C12: lattice results supply matrix elements; they are not tests of cross-section factorization.
* **Table `tab:lamet-status` — 第 22 章状态表.** Columns: label | statement (short) | tag (+ qualifier; content grade for R_cond) | premises / bottleneck | error type of the weakest step | refereed? Rows: every canonical statement of this chapter. Generated from the registry (§08 `### Ch22`) by the writer.
* **注 `rem:lamet-open` — 开放问题** (no tag). (1) A Zimmermann-level OPE theorem for Wilson-line operators (`asm:lamet-ope`); (2) written renormalizability of QCD + space-like static source (`asm:lamet-aux-renorm`); (3) a uniform twist expansion in Ioffe time even in a solvable model (`asm:lamet-uniform`; Liu's GN theorem covers only imaginary Ioffe time, Ch23); (4) exponential decay of hadronic Wilson-line correlators in a gauge theory with usable constants (`asm:lamet-large-z`, `asm:lamet-gap`); (5) the end-point exponent $\delta$ of $\rho_4$ (`cor:lamet-endpoint`); (6) GPDs at $x=\pm\xi$ (Ch23); (7) quasi-TMD factorization beyond arguments: a diagrammatic proof of (OL1) beyond one loop and of (OL2), a Glauber analysis for finite-length staples, the factorization of the large-momentum meson form factor used for $S_r$ (Ch23); (8) coordinate-space analyticity of DR Wightman functions (Ch23 `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt`); (9) higher orders: NNLO singlet/gluon kernels, NNLO momentum-space quasi-GPD kernels, three-loop heavy–light kernel (the N³LO non-singlet kernel exists, `Cheng:2024wyu`, `prop:lamet-twoloop`; A06 item (3)); (10) all-order twist-3 factorization with zero modes; (11) renormalon prescriptions: universality of $m_0(\tau)$; (12) check $\int d\xi\,C_{\rm hyb}=1$ for the higher-order hybrid kernels used in lattice fits; (13) the asymptotic (non-convergent) nature of the momentum-space power expansion (Ch23 O($N$) model); (14) colour-non-singlet DPDs and heavy-meson LCDA chains.

### Misconception box (Ch22)
C12 (lattice "verification"); M17 (the lattice CS kernel is non-perturbative input supplied through an [A] theorem); 「格点直接计算了 PDF 的 $x$ 依赖」 (no: Fourier/inverse step, `prop:lamet-slepian`, `prop:lamet-truncation`); 「LaMET 原理已被非微扰证明」 (no: `conj:lamet-principle` [C]); 「准 PDF 的矩等于 PDF 的矩」 (no: the $\overline{\rm MS}$ quasi-PDF has no moments; the pseudo-PDF has, `prop:lamet-tails`, `thm:lamet-coordinate`); 「匹配核依赖强子动量 $P^z$」 (no: parton momentum, `rem:lamet-early-form`); 「比值方案可直接用于动量空间匹配」 (`rem:lamet-ratio-large-z`).

# PART 1b — Part VII global decisions and annexes F1/F4/F5/F6 (verbatim)

## Part VII — global decisions (the former frontier §F0; binding for Ch22–Ch24)

### F0.1 Scope, budgets, format

| # | key | file | 中文标题（binding） | pp | format |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 22 | `lamet` | `ch22_lamet.tex` | LaMET 与格点因子化：季向东等人的因子化证明 | 18 | frontier |
| 23 | `yzliu` | `ch23_yzliu.tex` | Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 | 15 | frontier |
| 24 | `front` | `ch24_front.tex` | 其他前沿严格结果 | 18 | frontier |

Frontier format (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §11, §6.11): ≈55 % statements with hypotheses and proof architecture, ≈20 % explicit results (one-/two-loop kernels, checks, worked examples), ≈25 % status, evidence and open problems. Every chapter opens with the box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (§6.6) and ends with a status table and an open-problem list. No correspondence table is required (§6.7 lists only core chapters).

### F0.2 Ownership decisions (binding for this outline)

1. **Ch22 `lamet`** owns: the LaMET principle; lattice moments and the Euclidean Compton amplitude (v1 `prop:lattice-moments`); quasi-/pseudo-distributions and lattice cross sections (v1 `def:lattice-quasi`); support and analyticity; renormalization of straight space-like Wilson-line operators (v1 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); renormalization schemes incl. the hybrid scheme; one- and two-loop kernels; the matching theorems with the hypotheses (H1)–(H5) of the LaMET report (now `prop:lattice-renormalizability` and `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-uniform`, `asm:lamet-twist4`, `asm:lamet-large-z`) and the three proof routes (OPE route of Izubuchi–Ji–Jin–Stewart–Zhao; Ma–Qiu collinear-subtraction route; good lattice cross sections); v1 `thm:lattice-lamet`; target-mass and dynamical power corrections; the linear renormalon of the Wilson-line self-energy and leading-renormalon resummation; the Fourier step incl. the exponential-decay theorem (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2, VF-LAM-18); LaMET vs short-distance factorization (SDF); quasi-GPD/DA; **the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, the off-light-cone and reduced soft functions (definitions), the lattice Collins–Soper kernel (v1 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`) and TMD ratios**; v1 `rem:lattice-critiques`, `ex:lattice-quasi-tree`; the status board.
   - **Former deviation from the WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.1 default, adopted as binding at the merge** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.2 §6.11 item 5 and §12.2 now say the same; integrator decision MD9). Reason: `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` is a moved v1 `lattice` label, and the binding lead-author decision says that Ch22 absorbs the v1 `lattice` content with unchanged labels; its proof uses the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, and the no-forward-edge rule (§4.5 item 4) forbids `\uses{thm:<Ch23 label>}` in Ch22. The frontier task statement also lists "LaMET for DAs/GPDs/TMDs (Ji–Liu–Liu; Ebert–Stewart–Zhao; Vladimirov–Schäfer), lattice Collins–Soper kernel" under Ch22. Consequences: VF-LAM-15 and VF-LAM-16 are "Used in: Ch22" (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`); `fig:yzliu-leading-region` stays in Ch23 because it illustrates Liu's Collins-style proof route (`prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`).
2. **Ch23 `yzliu`** owns: Euclidean soft functions as heavy-quark (HQET) form factors; the partial-exponentiation lemma; pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle and the universality classes; the linearity (factorization) of rapidity divergences; the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ (on-light-cone definition, scheme independence, anomalous dimension, two-loop structure); the 2024 time-ordering theorem; Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem; the hard kernel (RGE, heavy–light decomposition, two loops); LFWF amplitudes; the meson form factor and the extraction of $S_I$; DY in terms of quasi-TMDs; Sivers ratios; the space-like jet function and its continuation; **the threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel** (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2 and VF-LAM-14); the anomalous-dimension algebra; the linear renormalon of the heavy–light kernel; GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; the exactly solvable 2D models; the extensions of the exponential-decay theorem.
3. **Ch22 does not own threshold factorization.** It owns only the one-loop endpoint structure of the matching kernel (`prop:lamet-oneloop`(e)) and the domain-of-validity remark (`rem:lamet-domain`), with forward pointers in running text to `thm:yzliu-threshold` (never in `\uses`).
4. **Ch24 `front`** owns all OTHERS.md items (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 5). It never re-states a core statement. Where a core statement already covers part of a topic, Ch24 states the frontier refinement under a new `front` label and cites the core label; the core statements keep their labels and owners. Overlaps handled this way: `thm:ir-landau-momentum`, `rem:ir-landau-status`, `prop:ir-coleman-norton`, `prop:ir-regions-minkowski`, `lem:appD-gordan` (Landau/regions); `thm:power-ope-convergence` (massive $\varphi^4_4$ OPE convergence stays in Ch21); `prop:scet-glauber-operators` (RS Glauber operators stay in Ch15); `prop:break-jet-veto`, `prop:break-fss`, `prop:break-spacelike`, `prop:break-mass-singularity`, `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18); `prop:jets-sll`, `thm:jets-ngl-factorization`, `thm:jets-bms` (Ch16); `thm:smallx-bk`, `thm:smallx-bfkl` (Ch17); `conj:heavy-nrqcd-production`, `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation` (Ch20); `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8); `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `rem:appJ-nonlocal`, `prop:appL-renormalized` (App J, App L); `prop:power-nlp-endpoint` (Ch21); `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13); `prop:jets-ngl-beyond` (Ch16); `prop:dy-abelian-model` (Ch12, the eikonal spectator phase); `thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay` (Ch20). Requested core-side pointers: §F5.
5. **Exponential decay.** Ch22 owns `thm:lamet-exp-decay` (the theorem of Ji–Liu–Su under axioms) and `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (the QCD asymptotic forms); Ch23 owns `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` (all-order spectral representation and exponential decay in $\varphi^3/\varphi^4$, [P]); the extension of `thm:lamet-exp-decay` to one-particle intermediate states is described in the text of that entry, with the status of `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, and is not graded separately.

### F0.4 Tags, qualifiers and assumptions in Part VII

1. **Mapping of frontier grade notations** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4): "[R] in PT, diagram by diagram" → [R, R_PT]; "[R]$_{1L}$/[R]$_{2L}$" → [R, R_PT] for the statement restricted to that order (first sentence states the order), the all-order statement keeps its own tag; "[R] (cond.)" → [R, R_cond]; "[R] under Wightman-type axioms" → [R, R_cond] with `asm:intro-wightman` + the frontier assumption it needs; "[R] (model)" → [P] with the model in the first sentence (the model-internal claim may get a full proof); "[R]†" → this outline decides (Ch24: [A] unless the proof is printed in full; status paragraph 「（证明仅见于预印本）」); sub-grades A±: dropped; "open" → [C].
2. **What becomes an `asm:`.** Only hypotheses that act as *formal premises* of a statement (R_cond premises, or hypotheses that several statements share) become register entries. The weakest *step* of an [A] argument (e.g. Liu's explicitly assumed IR-control step in the rapidity-slicing argument) is named in the 「地位与缺口」 paragraph, not turned into an assumption — otherwise the grade-propagation rule (§4.6) would turn every [A] argument with a sketched step into [C].
3. **Frontier register entries (14).** Ch22 (9): `asm:lamet-aux-renorm` [A], `asm:lamet-ope` [A], `asm:lamet-uniform` [C], `asm:lamet-twist4` [C], `asm:lamet-large-z` [C], `asm:lamet-gap` [C], `asm:lamet-staple` [A], `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` [A], `asm:lamet-order-limits` [A]. Ch23 (3): `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` [C], `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` [A], `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` [P]. Ch24 (2): `asm:front-mor-complete` [C], `asm:front-scet-modes` [A]. Full register entries with all fields: §F1. All are *technical* assumptions in the sense of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 7: they enter the grade propagation and fix the content grade of every R_cond statement that lists them (e.g. the explicitly non-perturbative `thm:lamet-exp-decay` and `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` have content grade [C]); only the six global `asm:intro-*` entries are exempt.
4. **Inner item names** are chapter-unique: (AX1)–(AX3) aux field; (OP1)–(OP2) OPE; (UI) uniformity; (TW1)–(TW2) twist 4; (LZ1)–(LZ2) large $z$; (GP1)–(GP3) gap; (ST1)–(ST3) staple; (SR) soft rapidity; (OL1)–(OL2) order of limits; (HP1)–(HP3) heavy pair; (WP1)–(WP2) Wightman PT; (UL) uniform light-cone limit; (MR) regions; (SM1)–(SM2) SCET modes. Local hypothesis lists inside statements: (QT1)–(QT7) (Liu's quasi-TMD power counting), (PS1)–(PS3) (positivity), (J1)–(J4) (Jantzen), (ED-a)–(ED-c) are not used (the decay theorem uses `asm:lamet-gap`). The frontier reports' own numbering (Thm 4.3, Prop 10.4, (H1)–(H6), (T1)–(T5), (Q1)–(Q7), (S1)–(S3)) is never printed.
5. **Non-perturbative status line.** Hadron-level statements end their hypotheses with 「非微扰地位：\cref{asm:…}（MA…）。」 using the global entries `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-bridge`; statements about partonic states, operators or models say 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」 or name the model.

### F0.5 How each entry is organised

As in v1 §0.7, plus the v2 fields: **type, canonical label, Chinese title, tag (+ qualifier; for R_cond the premise sentence)**; **statement** in formulas (our conventions; VF IDs in `%` comments); **hypotheses / non-perturbative line**; **`\uses{…}`** (only statement labels, only backward edges; v2-new core labels are those of REFDOC_ANALYSIS §5 and WRITER_SPEC_v2); **proof plan** (full / sketch, numbered steps, literature locations with reading depth); **status and gap** (for [A]/[P]/[C]/[V]: weakest step, `asm:` labels, error type where power counting is involved, refereed or preprint); **verification outcome** with claim ID (verify patch v2.3); **budget** in pages. "(数学，无标签)" marks pure mathematics.

## F1. Register entries of the frontier assumptions (14)

Format of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 3: statement (the inner items are those of the chapter entry, printed once by the owner); 「地位：」; 「文献：」; 「被使用于：」 (reverse dependencies, computed from the `\uses` lists of this outline; never copied into the assumption's own `\uses`); 「解除途径：」; `\uses`. The tag is the register's and is copied by the writer, never inferred (§4.4 item 2). All 14 are technical assumptions (§4.4 item 7); the six global `asm:intro-*` entries are owned by Ch1 and are not repeated here.

### F1.1 Ch22 `lamet` (9)

1. **`asm:lamet-aux-renorm` — 含类空辅助静态色源的 QCD 的可重整性** [A] · `sec:lamet-renorm`.
   - 陈述：(AX1)–(AX3) of the Ch22 entry (auxiliary colour-triplet spinless Grassmann field $Q$ along $\hat n=\hat e_z$; renormalizability of the extended theory with $Z_Q$, $Z_1^{QQg}$ (tied to $Z_g$) and $\delta m$ only, BRST-invariantly; constant $Q$ determinant in DR).
   - 地位：[A]-level — asserted in `Ji:2017oey` (the space-like extension of the HQET renormalizability argument is stated, not written), supported by the lattice auxiliary-field construction of `Green:2017xeu` and the coordinate-space degree counting of `Ishikawa:2017faj` (superficial degrees and "bookkeeping forests", not a BPHZ proof); explicit checks: two loops `Ji:2015jwa`, three-loop anomalous dimension `Braun:2020ymy`, heavy–light current to three loops `Chetyrkin:2003vi`. Decision D3 / A26: the multiplicative renormalizability of space-like Wilson-line operators is [A] in this book.
   - 文献：`Ji:2017oey`, `Green:2017xeu`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Dorn:1986dt`, `Dotsenko:1979wb`, `Braun:2020ymy`, `Chetyrkin:2003vi`.
   - 被使用于：`prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-gap`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`, `asm:lamet-staple`, `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`.
   - 解除途径：a written BRST-invariant renormalization proof of QCD + space-like static source (Zimmermann/BPHZ with the anisotropic power counting of the propagator $\theta(x^z)\delta(x^0)\delta^2(x_\perp)$, or flow equations in the style of `Frob:2015uqy`), including the treatment of $\det(i\hat n\cdot D)$ and of $\delta(0)$-type terms.
   - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst, def:prelim-wilson}`.
2. **`asm:lamet-ope` — 类空 Wilson 线算符的短距离 OPE（重整化微扰论）** [A] · `sec:lattice-matching`.
   - 陈述：(OP1) the leading-twist light-ray OPE of the renormalized operator $O^R_{\gamma^\alpha}(z,\mu)$ as $z^2\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$, with traceless symmetric twist-2 quark and gluon operators and coefficients $C_n$, $C'_n$; (OP2) for massless on-shell partonic states the leading-twist expression holds for all $z$ order by order.
   - 地位：[A] — argued by `IzubuchiEtAl2018` (read) from the auxiliary-field localization (the non-local operator becomes a product of local operators of the extended theory) plus the standard OPE; no Zimmermann-level theorem for Wilson-line operators is known — wording 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索，尚未见……」 (A06). The remark under `tab:lamet-dependency` lists what a proof needs.
   - 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018` §II, `Radyushkin2017`, `Braun:2007wv`, `Ji:2017oey`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-coordinate`, `thm:lamet-parton-momentum`, `prop:lamet-tmc`, `prop:lamet-sdf`, `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`.
   - 解除途径：Zimmermann's forest formula for the extended theory with the anisotropic power counting; or a flow-equation OPE theorem (cf. `Frob:2016mzv`) extended to theories with a static auxiliary field.
   - `\uses{prop:lattice-renormalizability, asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:dis-twist}`.
3. **`asm:lamet-uniform` — 扭度展开对 Ioffe 时间的一致性** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(UI-a) coordinate form — the twist-2 remainder of the hadronic ITD is bounded by $K(-z^2)\Lambda^2(1+|\ln(-z^2\mu^2)|)^k$ uniformly in $\lambda$ for $0<-z^2\le z_0^2$; (UI-b) ray form — along $z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$, $|\lambda|\le z_0P^z$, the remainder beyond twist 4 has Fourier transform $o(P_z^{-2})$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$.
   - 地位：[C] — no all-order argument; evidence from the renormalon model (`prop:lamet-bvz`) and from solvable models; Liu's Gross–Neveu theorem covers only imaginary Ioffe time (`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`, Ch23). The frontier report's hypothesis was reformulated (writer note in the Ch22 entry): a pointwise bound of type (UI-a) does not give a $P^z$-suppressed Fourier transform.
   - 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Braun:2018brg`, `JiEtAl2021`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：a non-perturbative OPE with remainder bounds uniform in $\lambda$ (cf. `asm:intro-np-ope`); as a first step a model proof at real Ioffe time.
   - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, prop:lamet-tmc}`.
4. **`asm:lamet-twist4` — 领头幂次修正的结构（扭度 4）** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(TW1) $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}=z^2\Lambda^2\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\rho_4(\alpha,z^2)$ with $\rho_4$ supported in $[-1,1]$, smooth inside; (TW2) at most logarithmic $z^2$ dependence of $\rho_4$.
   - 地位：[C] — the form is suggested by the twist-4 OPE in perturbation theory (support as in `prop:lamet-support`), the hadronic structure is assumed.
   - 文献：`Braun:2018brg`, `Radyushkin2017`, `JiEtAl2021`.
   - 被使用于：`asm:lamet-large-z`, `thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：a twist-4 OPE for hadron matrix elements with control of the $z^2$ dependence (non-perturbative OPE).
   - `\uses{prop:lamet-support, cor:lamet-entire}`.
5. **`asm:lamet-large-z` — 大距离区域的控制** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(LZ1) the region $|\lambda|>z_0P^z$ contributes $o(1)$ to the Fourier transform of $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$; (LZ2) the same with $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}-\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ is $o(P_z^{-2})$.
   - 地位：[C] — requires large-distance control of hadronic Wilson-line correlators with constants uniform in $P^z$; `thm:lamet-exp-decay` gives decay under `asm:lamet-gap` but no usable constants; `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`.
   - 文献：`Ji:2026vir` (预印本), `JiEtAl2021`, `Gao:2021dbh`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：quantitative exponential decay of hadronic matrix elements with constants uniform in $P^z$.
   - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-twist4}`.
6. **`asm:lamet-gap` — 谱隙与边界值正则性** [C] · `sec:lamet-fourier`.
   - 陈述：(GP1) isolated one-particle external state, other contributing states at least $\Delta m$ above it; (GP2) Fredenhagen-type regularity of the boundary values of the ordered two-point functions in complex time; (GP3) QCD application: non-perturbative reduction of the Wilson-line operator to local operators of QCD + static source and a gap $\Lambda_X$ above the static threshold.
   - 地位：[C] for QCD — (GP1), (GP2) are standard axiomatic hypotheses for the channel considered; (GP3) needs confinement in the heavy–light sector and a non-perturbative version of `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`; lattice values ($\Lambda_X\approx0.5$ GeV) are evidence only.
   - 文献：`Ji:2026vir` §5 (预印本, read), `Fredenhagen:1984bz`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`.
   - 解除途径：(GP1), (GP2) in a constructive or axiomatic setting for the channel; (GP3) requires control of confinement — beyond present methods.
   - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm}`.
7. **`asm:lamet-staple` — 订书钉形类空 Wilson 线算符的重整化与 $L\to\infty$ 极限** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(ST1) multiplicative renormalizability of the staple operator divided by $\sqrt{Z_E}$ with end-point factors only; (ST2) existence of $L\to\infty$ order by order (pinch poles cancel against $\sqrt{Z_E}$; ultrasoft modes cancel after resummation of dipole self-interactions); (ST3) on the lattice $\sqrt{Z_E}$ plus the short-distance matrix element removes all UV divergences; same-dimension mixing as in `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(b).
   - 地位：[A]-level — argued to all orders (Ch23 `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft` gives the all-order argument for (ST2), itself [A] with the ultrasoft cancellation argued, not proven); explicit one-loop checks; lattice renormalization studies.
   - 文献：`Ji:2019ewn`, `Liu:2022nnk` §§3–4, `Ebert:2022fmh`, `Ebert:2019tvc`, `Constantinou:2019vyb`, `Alexandrou:2023ucc`; history `Collins:2008ht`, `Ji:2018hvs` (A04).
   - 被使用于：`def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`.
   - 解除途径：the auxiliary-field renormalization proof (as for `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`) for staples with cusps, plus an all-order treatment of ultrasoft and "ultra-ultrasoft" modes.
   - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson}`.
8. **`asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` — 离光锥软函数的大快度结构** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(SR) $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$ with $\tilde K$ the Collins–Soper kernel.
   - 地位：[A]-level — the all-order argument is Ch23 `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear` (complete except an explicitly assumed IR step of the rapidity slicing); claimed in `CollinsSoper1981`; conformal-map renormalization theorem for light-like regulators (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`; Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`); explicit three-loop soft functions (`Li:2016axz`).
   - 文献：`Ji:2019sxk`, `Liu:2022nnk` App. E, `CollinsSoper1981`, `Vladimirov:2017ksc`, `Li:2016axz`.
   - 被使用于：`def:lamet-reduced-soft`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`.
   - 解除途径：a proof of the IR-control step of the rapidity-slicing argument (Ch23).
   - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
9. **`asm:lamet-order-limits` — 极限次序** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(OL1) $\varepsilon\to0$ commutes with $L\to\infty$ and with the off-light-cone rapidity regulator limit for $\Omega/\sqrt S$; (OL2) the large-rapidity and Collins schemes differ only in the order of $\varepsilon\to0$ and $y_B\to-\infty$, a UV effect absorbed into $C_q(\sqrt{\zeta_{\rm LR}}/2,\mu)$.
   - 地位：[A] — argued to all orders by `Ebert:2022fmh` ((OL1) verified at one loop; (OL2) by asymptotic freedom), not a diagram-by-diagram proof.
   - 文献：`Ebert:2022fmh` (read).
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`.
   - 解除途径：a diagrammatic proof of (OL1) beyond one loop and of (OL2).
   - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-collins}`.

### F1.2 Ch23 `yzliu` (3)

10. **`asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` — 静态色单态 $Q\bar Q$ 对的谱假设** [C] · `sec:yzliu-soft`.
    - 陈述：(HP1) isolated colour-singlet ground state of the static pair at fixed $\vec b_\perp$ with residual energy $\gamma^{-1}V(\vec b_\perp)$, gapped continuum; (HP2) non-zero, boost-invariant overlap of the pair interpolating field; (HP3) Wilson-line self-energies and the pair time evolution cancel against $\sqrt{ZZ}$ (LSZ-type amputation; perturbatively `lem:yzliu-partial-exp`).
    - 地位：[C] — non-perturbative spectral assumptions; (HP1) is supported by lattice static potentials (evidence).
    - 文献：`Ji:2019sxk` (read), `Liu:2022nnk` §4.1–4.2, App. A.
    - 被使用于：`cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor`.
    - 解除途径：(HP1) from a gap in the static colour-singlet sector (confinement); (HP2), (HP3) from an LSZ-type construction for static sources in non-perturbative HQET.
    - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc}`.
11. **`asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` — 维数正规化微扰论中的 Wightman 结构** [A] · `sec:yzliu-analyticity`.
    - 陈述：(WP1) order by order, in linear covariant gauges and DR at generic $\varepsilon\ne0$, gluon and ghost Wightman functions satisfy the spectral condition and local commutativity, so that their coordinate-space representations are boundary values of functions holomorphic in the (permuted extended) tubes, covariant under proper complex Lorentz transformations; (WP2) null separations on the same light-like line give scaleless contributions, so $\eta\to0^+$ may be taken at integrand level.
    - 地位：[A]-level — formally a consequence of the covariant operator formalism (`KugoOjima1979`) and of the Bargmann–Hall–Wightman theorem (`Streater:1989vi`), used by Liu ("the author believes in it", `Liu:2023pjb` §4 item 2); not proven as a precise statement for DR at generic $\varepsilon$ (indefinite metric, gauge-dependent Green functions). It is the bottleneck of `thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`.
    - 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` App. F, `Liu:2023pjb` §2 and App. B, `Ji:2023pba` App. C.2, `KugoOjima1979`, `Streater:1989vi`.
    - 被使用于：`thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`.
    - 解除途径：a proof that the DR parametric representations of all gluon/ghost Wightman functions with numerators are boundary values of holomorphic functions in the permuted extended tubes — e.g. by extending the positivity of $P_{ij}(\alpha)$ (`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`) and the below-threshold argument of `thm:yzliu-time-ordering` to all graphs.
    - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.
12. **`asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` — 光锥极限对 ${\rm Im}\,\theta$ 的一致性** [P] · `sec:yzliu-rapidity`.
    - 陈述：(UL) the light-cone limit $Y\to\infty$ of the single-time-ordered soft functions is uniform in ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in[-\pi,\pi]$.
    - 地位：[P] — verified perturbatively for ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in\{0,\pm\frac\pi2,\pm\pi\}$ only.
    - 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` §4.5–4.7, §6.
    - 被使用于：`prop:yzliu-universality-classes`.
    - 解除途径：a uniform bound on the $O(e^{-\theta})$ remainder in the strip at each order.
    - `\uses{def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle, thm:yzliu-analyticity}`.

### F1.3 Ch24 `front` (2)

13. **`asm:front-mor-complete` — 区域集合的完备性** [C] · `sec:front-regions`.
    - 陈述：(MR) for the expansion considered, the listed regions (essential facets, or the regions found after dissecting the polytope), each expanded and integrated over the whole domain, reproduce the asymptotic expansion to all orders — no hidden regions, no non-scaleless overlap contributions beyond those of `lem:front-jantzen`.
    - 地位：[C] — proven for Euclidean limits (`thm:ir-regions-euclid`) and for the one-facet case (`prop:front-lp-facets`); the facet recipe fails in Minkowski kinematics from three loops (`prop:front-hidden-regions`); no general argument.
    - 文献：`Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw` §6, `Gardi:2024axt`, `Jantzen2011`.
    - 被使用于：`prop:front-commuting`.
    - 解除途径：`conj:front-mor-minkowski`; an algorithm guaranteed to find all regions (dissecting the polytope) with a completeness proof.
    - `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-regions}`.
14. **`asm:front-scet-modes` — SCET 的模式完备性与 Glauber 模式的缺席** [A] · `sec:front-nlp`.
    - 陈述：(SM1) the listed hard, (anti-)hard-collinear, collinear, soft and soft-quark modes reproduce all regions at the stated power (zero-bin subtractions consistent); (SM2) Glauber modes do not contribute at that power (automatic for decays into colour-neutral states and for $e^+e^-$; for partonic coefficient functions of processes with incoming partons the statement concerns the partonic cross section only).
    - 地位：[A] — the standard SCET assumption (critic C6, M18), covered by the region analyses of the processes considered; `prop:front-hidden-regions` shows that completeness has to be checked case by case. Ownership note (§F5 item 11): it may move to Ch15 if the lead author wants `thm:scet-thrust` to cite it.
    - 文献：`Liu:2019oav`, `Liu:2020wbn`, `Beneke:2022obx`, `RothsteinStewart2016`.
    - 被使用于：`thm:front-hgg`, `prop:front-ggh`, `prop:front-nlp-ll`, `prop:front-nlp-dis`, `prop:front-sidis-nlp`.
    - 解除途径：a region analysis (pinch analysis and power counting, Ch4, Ch9) of each process at the stated power with the Glauber region treated explicitly.
    - `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.

Item 13 (REFS_NOTES_v2) waits for the verify patch. The original notes follow for the record.

App L: confirm that the proof of `prop:appL-renormalized` does not presuppose the non-local classification (circularity check of N04). App K: `thm:appK-muntz` is used by `prop:front-brst-bilocal`.

The lead author decides between (a) keeping [R, R_PT] for Ch21 with a first sentence naming the model 「在欧氏有质量 $\varphi^4_4$ 中，在固定圈数下，」 (a rigorous model-internal theorem) and (b) [P]; master-table row 68 follows.

11. **`asm:front-scet-modes` ownership.** It is a new register entry (no core SCET assumption exists among the 25 entries of refdoc S6). If the lead author wants Ch15 statements (`thm:scet-thrust`) to cite it, it moves to Ch15 under a new key before any writer starts (the label then changes; no writer has used it yet).
12. **Ch1 and Ch25.** `tab:intro-assumptions` lists the 14 frontier register entries; Ch25 copies `rem:lamet-open`, `rem:yzliu-open`, `rem:front-open` into its open-problem list and the rows of §F3 into the master table.

Keys added after INSPIRE verification: `Chou:2022drv` (E01), `Becher:2026kbr` (E16), `Nakanishi:1990qm`; Joglekar 1977 = `Joglekar:1976eb`, `Joglekar:1976pe` (cited by `Collins2011` §11.3.2).
14. **Composite-statement convention used here.** Where a statement has a fixed-order part and an all-order part, the registry tag is the first tag and fixed-order items carry 「（单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）」 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 rule 3). A later [R, R_PT] statement never lists such a composite [A] statement in its `\uses` (e.g. `prop:lamet-oneloop` computes its one-loop counterterm itself; `prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop` and `prop:yzliu-gpd-oneloop` cite only definitions); the non-perturbative item of the former `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet` became the separate `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` so that the [A] theorem does not depend on a [C] assumption.

# PART 4 — Assumption register entries used (ASSUMPTIONS.md §3, verbatim)

#### `asm:front-mor-complete` — 区域集合的完备性 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch24 `sec:front-regions`. Technical.
- 陈述：(MR) for the expansion considered, the listed regions (essential facets, or the regions found after dissecting the polytope), each expanded and integrated over the whole domain, reproduce the asymptotic expansion to all orders — no hidden regions, no non-scaleless overlap contributions beyond those of `lem:front-jantzen`.
- 地位：[C] — proven for Euclidean limits (`thm:ir-regions-euclid`) and for the one-facet case (`prop:front-lp-facets`); the facet recipe fails in Minkowski kinematics from three loops (`prop:front-hidden-regions`); no general argument.
- 文献：`Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw` §6, `Gardi:2024axt`, `Jantzen2011`.
- 被使用于：`prop:front-commuting` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：`conj:front-mor-minkowski`; an algorithm guaranteed to find all regions (dissecting the polytope) with a completeness proof.
- `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-regions}`.

#### `asm:front-scet-modes` — SCET 的模式完备性与 Glauber 模式的缺席 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch24 `sec:front-nlp`. Technical.
- 陈述：(SM1) the listed hard, (anti-)hard-collinear, collinear, soft and soft-quark modes reproduce all regions at the stated power (zero-bin subtractions consistent); (SM2) Glauber modes do not contribute at that power (automatic for decays into colour-neutral states and for $e^+e^-$; for partonic coefficient functions of processes with incoming partons the statement concerns the partonic cross section only).
- 地位：[A] — the standard SCET assumption (critic C6, M18), covered by the region analyses of the processes considered; `prop:front-hidden-regions` shows that completeness has to be checked case by case. Ownership note (§F5 item 11): it may move to Ch15 if the lead author wants `thm:scet-thrust` to cite it.
- 文献：`Liu:2019oav`, `Liu:2020wbn`, `Beneke:2022obx`, `RothsteinStewart2016`.
- 被使用于：`thm:front-hgg`, `prop:front-ggh`, `prop:front-nlp-ll`, `prop:front-nlp-dis`, `prop:front-sidis-nlp` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a region analysis (pinch analysis and power counting, Ch4, Ch9) of each process at the stated power with the Glauber region treated explicitly.
- `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.
